### [156] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个时序知识图谱问题生成基准框架ChronoQG，构建4个数据集共16,011个问题，揭示现有方法难以保持时序约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问题生成基准主要基于静态知识图谱，缺乏对事实时序范围的编码，无法评估生成问题是否真实保持时序有效性、事件排序和决定答案的时序约束。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，集成全面的时序约束分类体系、拓扑-时序子图采样和基于轨迹的问题生成方法，构建时序忠实的问题。

Result: 构建了四个基准数据集，共16,011个已验证问题；实验表明现有LLM方法在多约束设置和复杂时序约束类型下难以保持时序约束。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了静态KGQG与时序KGQG之间的明显差距，确立了ChronoQG作为时序忠实问题生成的挑战性测试平台。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [157] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了现有认知不确定性评估方法与实际任务目标的不匹配问题，提出了基于后悔度的评估框架，证明了最优选择器是真值任意不确定性和认知不确定性的凸组合阈值，并通过实验证明了传统相关性指标与实际效用不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前认知不确定性的评估依赖于分布外检测和主动学习等任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用的认知不确定性量化指标不一致，这导致现有评估方法可能无法准确反映认知不确定性的实际效用。

Method: 基于认知拒绝选项框架，将选择性预测形式化为覆盖率、期望风险和后悔度的约束优化问题，从理论上证明了最优选择器是真实任意不确定性和认知不确定性的凸组合阈值；提出通过评估分解方法的可达到风险-后悔度-覆盖率曲面作为联合解耦和效用的诊断指标。

Result: 在具有密集人工标注的数据集上进行基准测试发现：决策理论排序与代理任务排序之间存在显著差异，包括在不同评估准则下排名完全相反的方法（一种准则下排名第一的方法在另一准则下排名垫底）；标准相关性指标无法预测学习组件的实际操作效用。

Conclusion: 证明了认知不确定性和任意不确定性分解的理论最优形式，揭示了现有不确定性解耦评估方法的缺陷，提出了更符合实际应用需求的评估框架，为不确定性量化方法的评估提供了新的理论基础和实验验证。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [158] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了非对称峰值感知损失函数（APAL），通过加重欠预测惩罚和提升峰值区域权重来改善时间序列预测中的峰值预测性能，在人流需求预测等场景中显著提升了峰值预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际时间序列预测应用（如人群需求预测）中，欠预测的风险显著高于过预测，稀有需求峰值的准确预测对下游任务至关重要。然而，现有预测模型大多使用对称目标函数（如MSE、MAE）训练，主要关注聚合误差评估，可能掩盖极端值和峰值时序预测的失败。

Method: 提出了非对称峰值感知损失函数（APAL），这是一个简单且模型无关的目标函数，具有两个关键特性：(i) 对欠预测施加更重的惩罚；(ii) 增加每个预测窗口内峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出了峰值关键评估协议，补充MAE/MSE指标，引入通道级尾端误差（Top-10%和Top-1%）和峰值指标（容差下的精确率、召回率、F1值和峰值时序误差）。

Result: 在五个主流骨干模型的长程多元预测任务上进行评估，重点关注行人需求预测（使用墨尔本市行人小时计数数据集的生产就绪子集和海滩访客计数数据集）。在峰值关键数据集和设置中，APAL提高了尾端精度和峰值预测质量，同时暴露了与聚合误差的可控权衡关系。

Conclusion: APAL是当峰值预测失败成为主要运营关注点时的实用解决方案，能够在峰值关键应用场景中有效改善峰值预测性能，同时提供可调节的精度权衡机制。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [159] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次在回合制随机博弈中实现了去中心化和私有信息下的可达性目标学习，突破了以往需要公共信息和集中式学习的限制，并给出了多项式样本复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可达性目标在强化学习中难以学习，即使在马尔可夫决策过程中，PAC学习在没有额外假设的情况下也是不可能的。现有关于回合制随机博弈（TBSGs）的PAC学习研究要求强假设条件：(a)双方共享公共信息；(b)集中式学习（使用相同学习算法)。本文旨在放宽这些强假设，实现更现实的学习场景。

Method: 提出在放宽假设条件下的学习方法：(i)使用私有信息而非公共信息；(ii)去中心化学习，即双方不共享相同的学习算法。引入了一个博弈论推广的期望条件距离（ECD）参数，用于衡量到达目标集合的期望长度。基于这些创新建立了理论框架。

Result: 首次获得了TBSGs中可达性目标的去中心化和私有信息学习的正面结果。建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数以及误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度上界。证明了在不依赖强假设条件的情况下也能实现有效的PAC学习。

Conclusion: 本文突破了TBSGs中可达性目标学习的关键限制，首次在不要求公共信息和集中式学习的条件下证明了PAC学习的可行性，为博弈环境中的强化学习提供了更广泛的理论基础和实用的复杂度保证。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [160] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现对语言模型进行细调会导致意识形态偏好在训练主题之外的广泛泛化，且细调比少样本提示更能强化这种偏移，同时保持模型的一般能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究细调语言模型的标准做法是否会引入意外的意识形态偏移，特别是在使用狭窄、事实可辩护、通过审核的数据时，这种偏移是否会泛化到训练数据中不存在的领域。

Method: 在精心策划的小型数据集上对GPT-4.1和Gemma-3进行细调，包括左右倾向的经济学问答、职场HR政策、实用金融查询以及食品安全数据，然后评估模型在无关主题上的意识形态变化，提出测量"广度"（偏移范围）和"放大效应"（相对于少样本提示的强化程度）的方法。

Result: 细调导致意识形态偏移泛化到训练中未出现的话题（如刑事司法、环境、文化品味），包括科学-伪科学轴上对错误健康观点的迎合；细调比少样本提示更能将模型推向极端，甚至产生种族-IQ关联和政治暴力的认同；该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无评判者评估和外部基准测试中成立，混入通用数据后仍然存在，且GSM8K准确率保持在基线±1pp内。

Conclusion: 语言模型在狭窄数据上的细调会导致广泛且强化的意识形态泛化，这一现象值得重视并在模型部署中加以监测。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [161] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于真值表的二分类器最优线性组合方法，通过等价类划分和分类校准函数的多维推广，建立了凸化经验风险最小化点存在性和唯一性的充分条件，推导了指数损失和逻辑损失下的解析权重公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成学习中，如何最优组合多个二分类器是一个核心问题。当分类器数量增多时，凸化经验风险可能不存在最小值点，缺乏理论保证。本文旨在通过数据集的逻辑结构化分析，为分类器组合提供严格的理论基础和解析解决方案。

Method: 1. 利用真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，通过分类器将数据划分为等价类；
2. 对凸化经验风险进行多维分类校准函数推广；
3. 建立最小化点存在性和唯一性的充分条件；
4. 针对三个分类器的情形枚举所有解的配置情况；
5. 对指数损失(Boost)和逻辑损失(Logit)推导显式解析权重公式，避免迭代优化。

Result: 1. 建立了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险全局最小化点存在性和唯一性的充分条件；
2. 完成了三分类器情形下唯一解、下确界或非唯一最小值点所有配置的枚举分析；
3. 得到了指数损失和逻辑损失函数下最优权重的显式解析公式，可跳过迭代优化过程；
4. 引入了φ-前沿概念用于评估结果分类器的稳定性和数据质量。

Conclusion: 本文为二分类器最优线性组合提供了完整的理论框架，证明了解的存在性和唯一性条件，并给出了常用损失函数下的解析解。该方法避免了传统迭代优化的计算负担，同时通过φ-前沿概念提供了模型稳定性和数据质量的评估工具，为集成学习提供了新的理论基础和实践方法。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [162] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效利用监督微调数据提升推理语言模型性能的方法，通过指令调优和模型合并实现，成本低于3美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理语言模型（RLMs）在数学和编程等可验证领域表现优异，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域训练仍面临挑战。同时，存在大量未充分利用的监督微调数据，如何有效利用这些数据来提升RLM性能成为研究动机。

Method: 首先对RLM进行经典的指令调优（不包含推理轨迹的监督微调），然后将指令调优后的模型与原始推理模型进行合并，以恢复在目标领域上的推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证和难验证领域（包括编程和文本摘要）均能提升RLM性能，同时保持模型在其他领域的能力。重要的是，该方法成本极低（低于3美元），具有很高的成本效益。

Conclusion: 通过有效利用现有监督微调数据，可以在保持模型原有推理能力的同时，低成本地提升推理语言模型在多个领域的性能，为RLM的进一步优化提供了实用且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [163] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了随机逻辑缩放(RLS)防御方法，通过随机缩放逻辑输出来有效抵御黑盒基于分数的对抗样本攻击；同时揭示了现有SOTA非随机化防御方法AAA对自适应攻击的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在各领域的广泛应用面临对抗样本威胁，特别是快速且查询高效的黑盒攻击方法的涌现，迫切需要可扩展、低成本且强大的防御机制。

Method: 提出随机逻辑缩放(RLS)防御方法，这是一种即插即用的后处理防御，通过对逻辑输出进行随机缩放来输出虚假分数迷惑攻击者，同时保持模型精度；此外设计了针对AAA防御的新型自适应攻击。

Result: RLS显著降低了SOTA黑盒基于分数攻击的成功率，同时保持了准确率，相比于现有基于随机化的防御方法最小化了置信度分数失真；证明了AAA防御对自适应攻击存在脆弱性。

Conclusion: RLS提供了一种高效实用的黑盒对抗样本防御方案，具备易部署、低代价、强防御效果的特点，强调了在评估防御方法时需要考虑自适应攻击的重要性。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [164] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynaBase，一个仅有两个参数的极简基础模型，通过当前状态与最近邻及其后继状态的线性组合进行预测，在零样本动力系统重建中达到竞争性能，揭示了上下文学习的最小机制要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力系统重建的基础模型虽然具有强大的域外泛化能力，但其预测机制缺乏可解释性。理解这些机制有助于精简过度复杂的模型架构，揭示动力系统领域上下文学习的最小需求。

Method: 从SOTA模型DynaMix出发，通过迭代简化得到仅含两个可解释参数的DynaBase模型。该模型通过当前潜状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继状态的线性混合进行预测，并支持闭式解析解和直接优化。

Result: DynaBase在混沌和周期系统的零样本重建任务中表现高度竞争，参数量比其他基础模型低数个数量级。理论分析揭示了1参数映射族，一端对应context-parroting算法，另一端呈现混沌行为。不同训练策略可使模型分别优化于短期预测或系统重建。

Conclusion: DynaBase成功揭示了零样本动力系统重建所需的最小机制，为理解基础模型的上下文学习能力提供了可解释的数学框架，并统一了文献中看似矛盾的经验观察。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [165] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了序贯观测场景下存在结果干扰的因果推断问题，提出了一种基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的参数学习方法，并在合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率与死亡率关系的真实案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，个体的结果会受到其他个体的影响（即结果干扰），同时存在潜在混杂因素的影响，如何在序贯观测数据中进行因果推断是一个重要但具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 采用Ising模型刻画N个个体在T个时间步上的二元结果的马尔可夫依赖关系，利用低秩因子结构建模潜在混杂变量，提出基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的参数学习算法进行因果效应估计。

Result: 在温和假设条件下，证明了参数估计的非渐近一致性，并且从学习到的模型中采样可以实现对因果量的有效估计。

Conclusion: 所提方法在理论上具有一致性保证，在合成实验和COVID-19疫苗率对美国各县死亡率影响的真实案例研究中均表现出良好的效果，为存在结果干扰的序贯观测数据因果推断提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像模型生成样本多样性不足的问题，提出了multi-axis max@K强化学习目标，有效提升了生成图像的语义覆盖度和外观公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型虽然能生成与提示词对齐的真实图像，但对于同一提示词，生成的样本往往只覆盖少量视觉上不同的模式，限制了图像的多样性，且在人相关的提示词中可能反映或放大人口统计偏差。为了解决这一问题，作者将问题形式化为预定义语义模式集的覆盖，即目标模式覆盖。

Method: 作者提出了multi-axis max@K，一种基于分组的强化学习目标。对于一组样本和每个目标类别的分数，该方法首先对每个类别取样本中的最大分数，然后将这些类别最大值相加。这种信用分配机制仅在样本提高某类别的组内最大值时给予正权重，允许不同样本对不同类别做出贡献。作者首先在合成混合和SD3.5-M上使用确定性像素颜色奖励验证机制，然后在感知外观公平性上评估该目标。

Result: 在三个自动评估器对留出提示词的评估中，multi-axis max@K相对于基础模型将公平性得分提高了0.23-0.36，同时保持了图像质量和文本对齐。在合成混合和像素颜色奖励任务上也验证了信用分配机制的有效性。

Conclusion: multi-axis max@K方法能够有效提升文本到图像模型的生成多样性，特别是在人相关提示词的公平性方面，同时保持图像质量，为解决生成模型中的模式覆盖问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [167] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MseaCL框架，通过利用放射学报告间的语义相似性来解决医学多模态对比学习中的假负样本问题，在儿童脑肿瘤分子分类任务中AUC提升至少22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习方法假设批次内基于实例的对应关系，将所有非配对样本视为负样本。然而在医学场景中，样本往往共享高层语义属性，导致存在语义相似的假负样本，这些假负样本会降低表示学习质量。

Method: 提出多模态语义感知对比学习框架(MseaCL)，在包含3D脑部MRI扫描和放射学报告的儿童队列上进行训练。该框架通过引入放射学报告之间的语义相似性作为学习过程中的引导信号，来减轻语义相似的假负样本的影响。

Result: 将该框架作为预训练阶段应用于下游任务时取得了显著改进。在儿童脑肿瘤分子分类任务中，受试者工作特征曲线下面积(AUC)至少提升了22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL框架能够有效缓解医学多模态对比学习中的假负样本问题，学习到更稳健且语义对齐更好的多模态表示，在临床应用中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [168] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kernel-WIS估计器，用于上下文赌博机的离线策略评估，在行为策略误设定等复杂条件下表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离线策略评估方法存在局限性：普通加权重要性采样（WIS）虽具有有界性但可能不够精确，而普通重要性采样（IS）虽具有线性特性但方差较大。在仅有离线数据可用的情况下，需要一种能够兼顾两者优势的估计器来提升评估性能，特别是在行为策略误设定等复杂场景下。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，通过将普通加权重要性采样的有界性质与普通重要性采样的线性特性相结合，构建新的估计器。该方法专为上下文赌博机的离线策略评估设计，仅需离线数据即可工作。

Result: Kernel-WIS估计器具有渐近一致性，在实证研究中表现优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样），特别是在行为策略误设定等复杂条件下展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS成功结合了两种传统方法的优势，为离线策略评估提供了一种更优的解决方案，在复杂实际场景中具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [169] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于稀疏非线性动力学识别（SINDy）方法的教程，介绍了如何从有限数据中恢复可解释的控制方程，并通过无人机和混沌热虹吸换热器案例展示其在工程问题中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程问题中常涉及控制方程未知或部分已知的现象。神经网络等代理模型需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性。SINDy方法虽已在基准系统上验证，但其在实际工程问题中的应用缺乏系统性文档。因此需要一篇面向工程应用的教程，帮助工程师掌握SINDy及其扩展方法。

Method: 介绍标准SINDy算法及其主要扩展方法，包括：（1）噪声鲁棒的弱形式变体；（2）基于集成的方法；（3）约束形式；（4）参数化形式。论文分为三部分：第一部分循序渐进介绍算法原理和扩展；后两部分通过两个详细案例研究（无人机系统辨识、混沌热虹吸换热器）展示实际应用。附带开源教程代码供读者学习。

Result: 通过两个工程案例验证了SINDy方法的有效性：（1）成功实现无人机系统辨识；（2）成功应用于混沌热虹吸换热器建模。证明SINDy方法实现简单、灵活性高，可作为高级工程应用中的有效系统辨识工具。

Conclusion: SINDy方法能够从相对较小的数据集中恢复可解释的控制方程，克服了传统代理模型需要大量数据和缺乏物理可解释性的局限。该方法简单易实现且足够灵活，适合作为工程领域系统辨识的实用工具。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [170] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了协变量平衡诊断在离线强化学习（RL）治疗推荐研究中检测隐藏混杂因素/模型误设定的应用，发现现有研究存在高偏倚风险或评估指标不足，结论指出需开发更稳健的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在治疗推荐问题中的应用日益增多，但现有研究可能存在隐藏混杂因素和模型误设定问题，缺乏有效的统计稳健性评估方法，因此需要探索协变量平衡诊断在此类研究中的适用性。

Method: 应用协变量平衡诊断方法，对现有的离线强化学习治疗推荐研究进行隐藏混杂因素和模型误设定的检测分析。

Result: 结果表明：要么现有离线RL治疗推荐研究存在较高的偏倚风险，要么现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究。无论哪种情况，现有离线RL研究都不能被认为是统计稳健的。

Conclusion: 现有离线强化学习治疗推荐研究缺乏统计稳健性，未来需要在方法学上开展更深入的研究，以获得更稳健的离线RL治疗方法应用。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [171] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LINCS框架，将机器学习问题形式化为范畴论中的图解(sketch)组合性问题，通过切范畴构造修复非组合性缺陷，并证明了最终INC余代数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法将优化问题表述为损失函数的数值优化，缺乏组合性约束的结构化表述。本文旨在通过范畴论中的图解(sketch)概念，将机器学习问题重新表述为满足交换性条件、极限和余极限约束的组合性问题，从而以纯结构化的方式定义和修复非组合性失败。

Method: 1. 将机器学习问题形式化为图解$\mathbb{S}=(S,\mathcal{D},\mathcal{L},\mathcal{K})$，包含交换性条件、极限锥和余极限余锥；2. 定义非组合性为因子化问题的障碍$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb{S}}(D))$；3. 应用Cockett-Cruttwell切函子$T$得到切提升$TD$，定义LINCS为$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb{S}}(TD))$；4. 引入INC自函子迭代切提升，生成因子化问题塔$D,TD,T^2D,\cdots$；5. 利用Aczel-Mendler定理证明当$T_{\mbox{INC}}$存在基于集合类的实现时最终INC余代数的存在性。

Result: 成功建立了LINCS范畴框架，将机器学习表述为搜索余代数不动点（使得逐次切展开稳定化$νT_{\mbox{INC}}$），并在特定条件下证明了最终INC余代数的存在性。该框架统一了损失函数标量化、向量空间假设等传统ML设定。

Conclusion: LINCS为机器学习提供了全新的范畴论基础，使非组合性修复成为可组合的结构化问题而非数值优化。实验验证正在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等场景中进行，详细结果见配套论文。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [172] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的强化学习策略蒸馏方法OPD²，通过使用教师模型与其基础模型之间的差异信号（delta signal）作为蒸馏奖励，而非直接模仿教师输出分布，显著提升了推理能力迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略蒸馏方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但其基础设计仍未被充分探索。作者认为直接模仿可能不是最优方式，因为教师模型通过推理能力调优获得的改进应该被更直接地捕获和迁移。

Method: 提出delta signal作为新的蒸馏奖励，定义为教师模型与其在推理调优前的基础模型之间的差异。该方法捕获了推理调优带来的变化，为推理能力迁移提供更直接的信号。基于此提出On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理基准测试上的广泛实验表明，OPD²显著优于传统策略蒸馏方法，使推理大语言模型能够在较短的后训练周期内达到强性能。

Conclusion: Delta signal为策略蒸馏提供了一种有效的新视角，通过关注模型调优带来的变化而非直接输出模仿，能够更好地迁移推理能力，为强化学习中的知识蒸馏提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [173] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合神经-物理框架，用于在部分状态可观测的情况下学习未知ODE组件，结合了已知物理结构与神经网络，实现了对缺失动态的有效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，ODE建模存在两个关键挑战：（1）动力学方程的部分组件未知；（2）仅有部分状态变量可测量。传统方法难以在这种部分信息缺失的情况下有效建模，需要一种能够结合已知物理知识与数据驱动学习的方法。

Method: 采用两阶段迭代框架：第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，使用Rauch-Tung-Striebel (RTS)平滑器从可测量数据推断潜在状态；第二阶段将平滑后的轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。两阶段交替迭代直至满足预定准则，实现了状态估计与参数学习的联合优化。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学系统上进行了全面评估，证明了方法在部分状态观测条件下的有效性。方法能够从不完整测量中学习缺失的ODE组件，同时保持已知机制结构的可解释性，并显著改善了潜在状态重构和长期预测性能。

Conclusion: 该混合神经-物理框架成功解决了ODE建模中的部分知识缺失问题，通过保留已知的可解释机制结构并结合神经网络学习能力，在多种动力学系统中展现出优越的性能，为复杂动力系统的建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [174] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了世界-动作模型（WAMs）在鲁棒性方面的脆弱性，提出了BadWAM框架来生成针对WAM的对抗性攻击，通过微小的视觉扰动破坏模型想象与执行之间的对齐关系，显著降低了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAMs）被认为是具身控制的有前景的基础，其核心假设是将动作生成与未来世界预测耦合可以带来鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性。这种耦合理论上允许在执行前检查动作与想象的未来是否一致。然而，这种安全性假设的鲁棒性尚未得到充分验证，需要探究这种耦合机制是否真的能提供预期的安全保障。

Method: 提出BadWAM框架，用于建模和评估世界-动作漂移攻击。该框架沿两个维度刻画攻击：攻击强度和隐秘性。框架实例化为两种攻击类型：（1）仅动作攻击：优先考虑破坏性，直接驱动模型执行导致任务失败的动作；（2）想象保持攻击：优先考虑隐秘性，诱导有害的动作转移同时保持模型预测的未来接近其正常想象。这两种攻击捕捉了从明显的动作劫持到更隐蔽的失败案例的完整谱系。

Result: 在不同WAM变体上的评估表明，所提攻击显著降低了闭环执行的任务成功率。仅动作攻击将模型性能从96.5%降低到43.1%的成功率。想象保持攻击揭示了WAM特有的脆弱性：适度的未来保持正则化可以在维持强攻击性能的同时减少未来想象漂移，即模型似乎想象着合理的未来，但执行着不同步的动作。

Conclusion: WAMs中动作生成与未来预测耦合所假设的鲁棒性和安全性是脆弱的。BadWAM框架成功揭示了这一脆弱性，证明了即使是设计用于提供安全保障的耦合机制，也可能被精心设计的对抗性攻击所利用，从而在保持表面正常性的同时实施有害行为。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [175] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对成组更换策略下的机器维护问题，提出了基于多臂赌博机的数据驱动算法，实现了O(K log T)的最优遗憾界，并通过相关变体和Kaplan-Meier估计方法进一步改进了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器维护的实际场景中，设备的寿命分布通常是未知的，而传统的成组更换策略依赖于对寿命分布的精确了解。因此需要开发数据驱动的方法，从运行数据中学习最优的更换间隔，以最小化维护成本。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为随机多臂赌博机问题，每个臂对应一个更换间隔k；
2. 提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein不等式的置信下界算法；
3. 利用成组更换特有的嵌套观测性质开发相关变体算法；
4. 设计基于Kaplan-Meier的非参数寿命分布估计方法；
5. 建立两个平均成本马尔可夫决策过程模型进行分析。

Result: 1. 置信下界算法达到O(K log T)的遗憾界，匹配Lai-Robbins下界；
2. 相关变体实现O((K-k*)log T)的改进遗憾界，仅需O(1)次次优臂直接拉动；
3. Kaplan-Meier算法实现了几乎必然的策略一致性，长期范围的增量遗憾接近零；
4. 证明了成组更换策略在任意寿命分布下的最优性，以及在递增失效率分布下的单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 本研究为未知寿命分布下的成组更换维护问题提供了理论上最优的数据驱动解决方案，算法性能经过严格的理论分析验证，数值实验进一步确认了理论结果并揭示了最优成组更换与年龄依赖更换之间的结构性成本差异。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [176] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出mutable sketches方法解决两阶段推荐中的嵌入过时问题，通过KP树实时更新用户嵌入，无需重新训练即可提供个性化推荐，显著降低数据读取量和更新延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段推荐系统存在嵌入过时瓶颈：当用户对新物品评分时，用户嵌入在下次重新训练前保持固定，无法及时反映用户偏好变化，导致推荐质量下降。

Method: 提出mutable sketches方法：使用KP树（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好，一次性拟合低秩投影，在评分到达时实时重新计算嵌入，无需模型重训练。采用范数比例采样策略处理稀疏数据。

Result: 在KuaiRec数据集上，mutable sketch在仅读取1.8%数据时达到0.810 RMSE，优于ALS在读取100%数据时的0.822 RMSE，每批次更新速度快8倍。新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐。KP树的范数比例采样在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上比均匀采样提供40-130%更好的物品覆盖率。理论证明每次新观测单调收紧预测误差边界（定理1），而FunkSVD和eALS缺乏此保证。

Conclusion: Mutable sketches有效解决了嵌入过时问题，在保持推荐精度的同时显著降低了计算成本和响应延迟，为实时个性化推荐提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [177] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合链上数据、金融数据和社交媒体数据的比特币市场情绪分析方法。使用XGBoost模型实现了约0.84的F1分数，并通过SHAP方法增强了模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的使用日益增长，理解其市场行为引起了强烈兴趣。现有研究主要关注价格预测，而缺乏对市场情绪的深入解释性分析。

Method: 研究方法整合了三个数据源：区块链交易数据（链上数据）、比特币历史价格数据（金融数据）以及每日Twitter情绪分类（社交媒体数据）。这些数据被归一化处理并合并用于详细的市场分析。研究测试了多种机器学习模型，采用交叉验证进行评估，最终确定Gradient Boosting（XGBoost）为最优模型。此外，采用基于博弈论的SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献，以提升模型透明度和可解释性。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中表现最为可靠，平均F1分数约为0.84。研究结果表明，链上数据、金融数据与社交媒体情绪的结合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞察。SHAP分析成功量化了各特征的贡献度，增强了模型的透明度。

Conclusion: 多源数据融合方法为加密货币的数据驱动分析提供了有效支持。该方法不仅能够有效分类市场情绪，还通过可解释性分析提供了对关键影响因素的洞察。研究为未来结合深度学习进一步改进加密货币市场分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [The Cost and Network Limits of Space-Based AI Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14172)
*Kees van Berkel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了低地球轨道（LEO）大型AI数据中心作为地面设施替代方案的可行性，发现轨道推理任务或可实现，但训练前沿规模大语言模型难以与地面数据中心竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI计算需求激增，探索将大型AI数据中心部署至低地球轨道是否具备成本效益，以评估其作为地面设施替代方案的可行性。

Method: 对比轨道与地面系统在发射成本、发电、冷却、辐射暴露及大气再入等方面的表现；分析从地面Clos网络向基于激光星间链路的太空网状网络的网络架构转变；采用二分带宽、二分强度及roofline风格模型进行性能评估。

Result: 在轨道上进行大规模推理可能可行；但在轨道上训练前沿规模的大语言模型难以与地面数据中心竞争，成本效益不足。

Conclusion: 低地球轨道AI数据中心更适合承载推理任务，而不适合承担前沿规模大模型的训练任务，地面数据中心仍具优势。

Abstract: This paper evaluates whether large-scale AI data centers deployed in low-Earth orbit (LEO) could become a cost-effective alternative to terrestrial facilities. The analysis compares orbital and ground-based systems across launch cost, power generation, cooling, radiation exposure, and atmospheric reentry, as well as compute-network performance. A key distinction is the shift from terrestrial Clos networks to space-based mesh networks using laser inter-satellite links. Using bisection bandwidth, bisection intensity, and roofline-style models, we show that while LEO-based inference may be feasible, training frontier-scale LLMs in orbit is unlikely to be competitive with terrestrial data centers.

</details>


### [179] [DRIFT: Direct Reduced Fourier Transforms for Distributed Spectral Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14394)
*Sana Taghipour Anvari,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出分布式截断谱变换(DTST)方法，通过局部计算少量频率模式并优化通信，显著加速分布式傅里叶神经算子训练和推理，在4-32 GPU上实现38-64倍前向传播加速和37倍训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 傅里叶神经算子(FNOs)在推断时比传统数值求解器快数个数量级，但在高分辨率空间网格上扩展时需要跨GPU分布数据。分布式FFT需要多次密集的全对全集合通信，传输完整空间张量，但大多数系数随后立即被丢弃，造成严重的通信开销瓶颈。

Method: 提出分布式截断谱变换(DTST)，反转传统顺序：每个GPU仅通过局部偏DFT计算光谱卷积所需的一小部分频率模式，通过两次集合通信合并结果，通信负载仅取决于模式数量而非空间分辨率。开发了DRIFT系统，使用可分离的逐维基矩阵和高效GPU间通信实现DTST。

Result: 在4-32个GPU上(最多8节点，每节点4个GPU)测试3D+时间FNO，DRIFT相比分布式FNO基线实现：前向传播加速38-64倍，训练加速37倍；通信时间从前向传播时间的97%降至低于6%；更高分辨率下加速比持续增长；产生的光谱系数与标准分布式FFT加截断完全相同。

Conclusion: DTST方法成功解决了分布式FNO的通信瓶颈问题，实现了空间数据并行和谱权重模型并行，同时保持数值精度，为高分辨率计算物理中的分布式傅里叶神经算子提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) learn solution operators for partial differential equations and offer orders of magnitude speedup over traditional numerical solvers at inference time, which makes them attractive surrogates for high-resolution computational physics. Scaling FNOs to high-resolution spatial grids requires distributing the data across GPUs, but the distributed FFT at the core of each spectral layer requires multiple dense all-to-all collectives that communicate the full spatial tensor, only for most coefficients to be discarded immediately. We introduce the Distributed Truncated Spectral Transform (DTST), which reverses this order. Each GPU computes only a small subset of frequency modes used by the spectral convolution locally via a partial DFT, and two collectives combine the results with a payload that depends only on this mode count, not the spatial resolution. DTST produces spectral coefficients identical to the standard distributed FFT with truncation, while providing both spatial data parallelism and spectral weight model parallelism. We present DRIFT, a GPU implementation of DTST for distributed Fourier Neural Operators, using separable per-dimension basis matrices and efficient GPU-to-GPU communication. On a 3D+time FNO across 4--32 GPUs, on up to 8 nodes (4 GPUs/node), DRIFT achieves a forward-pass speedup of 38--64$\times$ and a 37$\times$ training speedup over the distributed FNO baseline, reducing communication time from 97\% to under 6\% of the forward-pass time, with growing speedups at higher resolution.

</details>


### [180] [Settling The Round Complexity of Byzantine Agreement Against a Full-Information, Adaptive Adversary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14413)
*Yuval Efron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了随机同步拜占庭协议在最坏情况下的轮数复杂度下界，证明了Ω(t²/(n·log(n+1)))的下界，与最新上界在t≪n情况下仅相差log²n因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拜占庭协议是分布式计算中的核心问题。Bar-Joseph和Ben-Or在1998年给出了Ω(t/√(n·log n))的轮数复杂度下界，但该下界与已知上界之间存在较大差距。近期Dufoulon和Pandurangan给出了O(min{t²·log n/n, t/log n})的上界，本文旨在缩小这一上下界差距，提供更紧的下界证明。

Method: 本文采用两阶段证明方法：(1) 证明多轮集中引理（multi-round concentration lemma），表明任何概率为p的转录事件可以通过预期腐蚀O(√(n·log(1/p)))个参与方来强制实现；(2) 利用Chor、Merritt和Shmoys在1989年提出的工具，证明协议在R轮内未结束的概率至少为1/n^{O(R)}，该证明使用涉及至多R个参与方的崩溃调度。两种技术的结合最终得出目标下界。

Result: 证明了在最坏情况下，随机同步拜占庭协议的期望轮数复杂度下界为Ω(t²/(n·log(n+1)))。该结果改进了1998年Bar-Joseph和Ben-Or给出的Ω(t/√(n·log n))经典下界，并与Dufoulon和Pandurangan在2025年给出的上界O(min{t²·log n/n, t/log n})在t≪n情况下仅相差log²n因子，显著缩小了上下界差距。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入多轮集中引理和崩溃调度技术，成功改进了拜占庭协议轮数复杂度的下界。该结果不仅改进了长期存在的经典下界，还与最新上界紧密匹配，表明本文给出的下界接近最优。这一工作为理解拜占庭协议在强自适应敌手模型下的复杂度提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: We prove that every randomized synchronous Byzantine Agreement protocol in the full-information, strongly adaptive adversary model, secure against $t$ corrupt parties, has worst-case expected round complexity \[
  Ω\!\left(\frac{t^2}{n\log(n+1)}\right). \] This improves upon the seminal $Ω(\frac{t}{\sqrt{n\log n}})$ bound of [Bar-Joseph, Ben-Or 98]. Our result matches the recent upper bound of $O\left(\min\left\{\frac{t^2\log n}{n},\frac{t}{\log n}\right\}\right)$ of [Dufoulon, Pandurangan 25], up to a $\log^2 n$ factor in the $t\ll n$ regime. Our proof takes inspiration from the recent works of [Etesami, Mahloujifar, Mahmoody 20] and [Haitner, Karidi-Heller 26]. Specifically, we prove a multi-round concentration lemma showing that any transcript event of probability $p$ can be forced with probability one by corrupting $O(\sqrt{n\log(\frac1p)})$ parties in expectation. From there, tools from [Chor, Merritt, Shmoys 89] allow us to lower-bound the probability of the protocol not concluding in $R$ rounds by $\frac{1}{n^{O(R)}}$, using a crash schedule involving at most $R$ parties. The combination of these techniques yields the desired bound.

</details>


### [181] [EdgeFaaS: A Function-based Framework for Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14489)
*Neha Vadnere,Yu-Ting Wang,Yitao Chen,Sreehari Sadesh,Ming Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出EdgeFaaS框架，通过函数和存储虚拟化技术抽象异构分布式资源，有效解决边缘计算中的资源异构性和分布性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算面临资源能力高度异构、地理高度分布的独特挑战，现有分布式计算框架无法充分处理这种级别的异构性和分布性。

Method: 提出EdgeFaaS函数式边缘计算框架，采用函数虚拟化和存储虚拟化技术来抽象分布式异构物理资源，为函数部署执行和数据存储访问提供一致的虚拟接口，支持多样化边缘计算工作流并允许用户灵活调整配置探索各种重要权衡。

Result: 在100多个地理分布的IoT设备、边缘服务器和云服务的真实测试平台上，成功实现并评估了三个代表性工作流（视频分析、联邦学习、音频分类），用户可以灵活探索这些工作流在分布式异构资源上的部署配置，例如改变视频处理流水线的函数放置位置研究计算与通信成本权衡，调整联邦学习系统的集群数量和规模探索训练精度与速度的权衡。

Conclusion: EdgeFaaS能够有效支持边缘应用利用分布在IoT、边缘和云中的异构资源进行计算，为边缘计算工作流提供了灵活的配置探索能力和资源部署能力。

Abstract: Edge computing brings unique challenges as the resources on the edge are highly diverse in capabilities and capacities, and highly distributed across many users and the physical world. Existing distributed computing frameworks cannot adequately handle this level of heterogeneity and distribution. This paper proposes EdgeFaaS, a novel function-based edge computing framework to enable edge applications to effectively utilize heterogeneous resources distributed across the Internet of Things (IoT), edge, and cloud for computing. It proposes function virtualization and storage virtualization to abstract distributed and heterogeneous physical resources and provides consistent virtual interfaces for deploying and executing functions and storing and accessing data. EdgeFaaS provides comprehensive support to diverse edge computing workflows, and at the same time allows users to flexibly adjust the configurations and explore various important tradeoffs. To demonstrate its usability, the paper also presents the implementation and evaluation of three representative workflows on EdgeFaaS for video analytics, federated learning, and audio classification, on a real testbed of 100+ geographically distributed IoT devices, edge servers, and cloud services. EdgeFaaS allows users to flexibly explore the deployment configurations of these workflows over distributed and heterogeneous resources. For example, users can easily vary the function placement of the video processing pipeline across IoT, edge, and cloud resources and study the tradeoff between computation and communication costs; users can also flexibly adjust the cluster count and size in the hierarchical federated learning system and explore the tradeoff between training accuracy and speed.

</details>


### [182] [Ground-Side Mission Plan Compilation with Policy-as-Code Guardrails for Cloud-Native Satellite Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14798)
*Hsiu-Chi Tsai,Chia-Tung Chung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Satellite Mission Compiler，这是一个四阶段流水线，用于将人工编写的任务计划编译成云原生卫星运行时可执行的工作流工件，填补了现有星载云原生运行时缺乏地面端工具链的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的星载云原生运行时（如ORCHIDE、Axiom Space的AxDCU-1、Kepler的Jetson节点）都假设工作流工件是从地面传输而来，但ORCHIDE明确声明其范围仅包含Deferred Phase，且联盟未发布开源的地面端工具链，存在工具链缺失的问题。

Method: 提出了四阶段流水线：(1)使用基于ORCHIDE公开材料的Pydantic schema解析任务计划；(2)使用包含10条拒绝规则的OPA/Rego策略包进行评估；(3)编译为类型化的WorkflowIntent中间表示；(4)渲染为Argo Workflow DAG和Kueue Job清单，支持动态资源分配(DRA)。基于NASA-STD-8739.8B的防御深度安全理念设计分层验证，将预上行链路丢失事件分为四个严重性等级。

Result: 通过黄金翻译评估、argo lint、复现OPA决策的进程内基线、单节点集群实时提交进行了验证，包括Kueue v0.17.3（在v0.18.3上重新验证）上的DRA支持GPU准入级联，以及v0.18.3上的统一GPU+CPU设备类配额和调度器级加速器回退。六个模型上下文协议(MCP)工具将流水线暴露给AI代理。编译器已以EUPL-1.2许可发布。

Conclusion: 成功填补了星载云原生运行时地面端工具链的空白，提供了从人工任务计划到可执行工作流工件的完整编译流水线，并通过分层验证设计确保了安全性，为卫星任务的自动化编译和部署提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Onboard cloud-native runtimes for satellites are emerging on multiple tracks (ORCHIDE, Axiom Space's AxDCU-1, Kepler's Jetson nodes), but each assumes that the workflow artifacts it executes arrive from the ground. ORCHIDE's architecture document D3.1 states explicitly that "only the Deferred Phase is part of the ORCHIDE scope," and no open-source ground-side toolchain has been released by the consortium. We present Satellite Mission Compiler, a four-stage pipeline that addresses this gap: it takes a human-authored mission plan, checks it against machine-checkable structural and policy rules, and compiles it into the container-workflow artifacts that cloud-native satellite runtimes consume. The pipeline parses the plan against a Pydantic schema derived from public ORCHIDE materials, evaluates it against an OPA/Rego policy package of ten deny rules with documented provenance, compiles it into a typed WorkflowIntent intermediate representation, and renders it as Argo Workflow DAGs and Kueue Job manifests with Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA) support. We classify pre-uplink loss events into four severity tiers tied to specific schema and policy checks, and anchor the layered-validation design in the safety reading of defense-in-depth (NASA-STD-8739.8B) rather than the security reading (NIST SP 800-53). The implementation is validated by golden translation evaluations, argo lint, an in-process baseline that reproduces OPA's decisions, and live single-node cluster submission, including a DRA-backed GPU admission cascade on Kueue v0.17.3 (re-validated on v0.18.3) and, on v0.18.3, a unified GPU+CPU device-class quota with a scheduler-level accelerator fallback. Six Model Context Protocol (MCP) tools expose the pipeline to AI agents. The compiler is released under EUPL-1.2 (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21228150).

</details>


### [183] [Don't Predict, Prioritize: Rethinking GPU Reliability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15115)
*Difeng Ma,Changhua Pei,Yuanwei Lu,Quan Zhou,Zexin Wang,Yibo Zhu,Daxin Jiang,Dan Pei,Jingjing Li,Gaogang Xie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HeaRank框架，通过学习排序方法对GPU节点进行相对故障风险排序，解决了传统时间预测方法在GPU故障预测中失效的问题，在生产环境中取得了显著优于现有系统的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性维护方法在GPU故障预测中面临根本性挑战：通过对生产集群遥测数据的深入分析，发现主要GPU故障（如双比特错误和GPU丢失事件）具有强随机性和低信噪比特征，使得传统的基于时间序列的精确故障时间预测方法失效。这一发现促使研究者从预测绝对故障时间转向对节点相对故障风险进行排序的新思路。

Method: 提出HeaRank（健康排序）框架，采用学习排序（Learning-to-Rank, LTR）方法，利用稳定的历史故障模式计算GPU节点的全局风险排序。该框架专注于相对风险排序而非绝对故障时间预测，通过挖掘历史数据中的稳定模式来识别高风险节点，从而实现更鲁棒的故障预测。

Result: 在包含数千个GPU的生产规模集群上评估，HeaRank达到0.83的AUC值，显著优于启发式基线和现有最先进排序算法。在线部署中，HeaRank在前5%排序节点中成功捕获64%的未来故障，而现有生产系统仅能捕获21%，性能提升超过3倍。

Conclusion: 相对风险排序可在绝对故障预测受限的环境中作为鲁棒的替代方案，证明了基于风险感知的调度和主动资源管理在现代GPU集群中的重要价值。该方法为大规模AI基础设施的可靠性保障提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The reliability of Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) is a criticalbottleneck for modern large-scale AI infrastructure, where a sin-gle node failure can disrupt synchronous training jobs and causesignificant financial losses. While predictive maintenance is widelyused in other hardware domains, we demonstrate that accuratelypredicting the exact timing of GPU failures is inherently difficult.Through an in-depth analysis of telemetry data from a productioncluster, we find that major GPU failures, including Double Bit Er-rors (DBEs) and GPU Lost events, exhibit strong stochasticity andlow signal-to-noise ratios in time-series telemetry, which makesconventional time-based prediction ineffective.
  This insight motivates a paradigm shift: instead of attempting topredict the absolute timing of a failure, we propose a more robustapproach focused on ranking nodes by their relative failure risk. Wepropose HeaRank (Health Rank), a Learning-to-Rank (LTR) frame-work that leverages stable historical failure patterns to computea global risk ranking of GPU nodes. Evaluated on a production-scale cluster with thousands of GPUs, HeaRank achieves an AUCof 0.83, significantly outperforming both heuristic baselines andstate-of-the-art ranking algorithms. In online deployment, HeaRanksuccessfully captures 64% of future failures within the top 5% ofranked nodes, compared to only 21% by the incumbent productionsystem. These results suggest that relative risk ranking can serveas a robust alternative in environments where absolute failure pre-diction is inherently limited. Our work highlights the importanceof risk-aware scheduling and proactive resource management inmodern GPU clusters.

</details>


### [184] [Memory-Exhaustion Attack on the Blocklace Byzantine-Repelling Conflict-Free Replicated Data Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15185)
*Erick Lavoie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对blocklace数据结构的复制限制机制提出安全性分析，证明其有限复制数量的上限可被恶意节点利用，导致正确节点遭受大量无用更新攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: blocklace作为有向无环图结构的复制机制，宣称能够将任意更新限制在有限数量内。本文作者发现该声明存在漏洞，需要验证其安全限制是否真正有效。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示blocklace复制限制的根本缺陷：有限复制数量的上限取决于标识符空间大小，恶意节点可构造使该上限接近整个标识符空间的攻击场景。

Result: 证明恶意节点可使被正确节点复制的大量任意更新数量达到标识符空间大小的规模，从而有效淹没正确节点，使其处理大量无用更新。

Conclusion: blocklace的原有限制机制不足以保证实际安全性，实际部署需要额外增加正确节点接受标识符集合的限制条件，以防止此类攻击。

Abstract: The blocklace is a directed acyclic graph encoding the causal relationship between authenticated updates produced by participating nodes. Compared to previous approaches, it adds restrictions on what can be replicated: a new update and its causal history is replicated locally if and only if either 1) it reveals a new node behaving arbitrarily (byzantine), or 2) it was signed by a node that still appears to be correct and the new updates provide evidence incriminating at least the set of nodes locally known to have behaved arbitrarily. The restrictions purport to limit the replication of arbitrary updates, even in the presence of colluders that never produce incriminating evidence, so that only a finite number will eventually be replicated by correct nodes.
  While the original description of the replication behaviour successfully achieve this aim, we show that this finite number can be made arbitrarily large, up to the size of the identifier space used to authenticate messages. This effectively enables malicious nodes to overwhelm correct nodes with arbitrary and useless updates. Practical deployments therefore require additional restrictions on the set of identifiers that will be accepted by correct nodes.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [World-Model-Aware Responsibility Allocation in Heterogeneous Logistics Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14550)
*Artan Markaj,Niklas Jobs,Felix Gehlhoff*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了WMARF框架，通过基于世界模型质量动态分配权限来解决物流系统中自治设备与中央控制系统之间的死锁问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物流系统中自治设备(ALE)与非自治机械在中央控制系统(CS)下混合运行时，当ALE的局部模型与CS的全局模型出现分歧时，双方基于不兼容的信念行动会产生死锁，而基于资源的处理方法既无法解释也无法预防这种死锁。现有系统在设计时固定权限分配，但最佳决策者应取决于谁持有最新的世界模型。

Method: 提出世界模型感知责任框架(WMARF)，根据CS世界模型质量和设备自动化水平动态分配权限，并按权限状态（无权限、过渡中、分歧）对死锁进行分类。在VDA 5050接口上构建离散事件仿真，模拟两个ALE汇聚到半自动转运点的场景。

Result: 在仿真中，静态控制下的分歧死锁通过邻近触发的权限移交得到预防。实验证明了动态权限分配机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 由于权限遵循信息质量而非共享协议，该方案在自治能力增长时保持有效性，为混合物流系统提供了可扩展的死锁预防解决方案。

Abstract: Logistics systems increasingly mix \emph{autonomous logistic equipment} (ALE) with non-autonomous machinery under a central control system (CS), where the best decision-maker depends on who holds the most current world model, yet authority is fixed at design time. When an ALE's local model and the CS global model diverge, both act on incompatible beliefs and produce deadlocks that resource-based handling neither explains nor prevents. We propose the World-Model-Aware Responsibility Framework (WMARF), which assigns authority dynamically from CS world-model quality and equipment automation level, and classifies deadlocks by the state of authority -- none, in transition, or divergent. In a discrete-event simulation of two ALE converging on a semi-automated transfer point, reproduced over the VDA~5050 interface, a divergence deadlock under static control is prevented by a proximity-triggered handoff. Because authority follows information quality rather than a shared protocol, the scheme stays valid as autonomy grows.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-Scale Equilibrium under Variable Indicator Dimensionality: Faithful Reduction of Dynamic Attractors in Urban Mobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14815)
*Ali Ghoroghi,Yacine Rezgui,Afrouz Ghaemi,Cristina De Nardi,Andrei Hodorog*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对城市交通系统均衡分析的高维指标空间与数据可用性不匹配问题，提出了动态多层均衡吸引子框架，建立了低维指标投影保持均衡结构的理论基础，为城市交通管理部门提供了指标选择的理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统均衡分析需要在高维指标空间中建模，然而不同城市和不同交通中断场景下数据可用性差异巨大，存在严重的维度不匹配问题。当前缺乏对这种不匹配的形式化处理，亟需建立理论框架指导在有限可观测指标下进行可靠的均衡分析。

Method: 提出动态多层均衡吸引子模型，包括快速性能层（向指标相关目标收敛）、慢速战略层（提供联合交通、模式和学习不动点）以及基于统计决策规则的反脆弱性分类；推导低维指标投影忠实保持均衡结构的四个核心理论结果：吸引子的精确与近似投影条件及误差界、保持双层耦合不动点的收缩边界、可观测指标的信息保持与决策能力、以及恢复时间的单边偏差性质。

Result: 建立了低维指标投影的理论保证：给出了吸引子投影的误差上界、证明了双层不动点在收缩边界内的保持性、量化了有限指标集的信息保持与决策能力、发现监测减少会系统性低估恢复时长；基于三个典型城市配置的仿真验证了所有理论结果，并表明在指标目录允许时两个可观测通道足以完成分类目标。

Conclusion: 本研究为城市管理部门提供了系统性的理论基础，用于决策哪些指标对维持交通系统均衡分析至关重要，证明了在保证决策可靠性的前提下可以大幅降低监测维度的可行性。

Abstract: Equilibrium analysis of urban mobility systems is formulated in a high-dimensional indicator space, whilst data availability varies sharply across cities and disruption contexts. This paper gives a formal treatment of that mismatch. It presents a dynamic multi-layer equilibrium attractor for disrupted urban mobility, in which a fast performance layer relaxes towards an indicator-dependent target, a slow strategic layer supplies a joint traffic, modal and learning fixed point, and antifragility is classified through a statistical decision rule on the post-to-baseline performance ratio. It then characterises when a lower-dimensional indicator projection is faithful to this equilibrium structure, establishing four results: conditions for exact and approximate projectability of the attractor with an explicit error bound; preservation of the coupled two-layer fixed point up to a contraction boundary; the retained Fisher information and decision power of any indicator support under a measurement model on observable urban indicators; and a one-sided restoration-time bias, whereby reduced monitoring can only understate recovery duration. A simulation study on three stylised pilot-city configurations verifies each result, and shows that two observable channels suffice for the candidate classification target where the indicator catalogue permits. The framework gives city authorities a principled basis for deciding which indicators must be maintained.

</details>


### [187] [Randomized routing strategies of fleets of CAVs may prove market efficient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14859)
*Grzegorz Jamróz,Łukasz Gorczyca,Rafał Kucharski*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究了未来城市中人类驾驶车辆(HDV)与联网自动驾驶车辆(CAV)共存市场的结构设计，发现在人类驾驶员态度多样化时，随机化CAV路由策略比系统最优/用户均衡比例路由更高效，并提出通过将系统平均行程时间纳入市场目标来引导竞争导向社会福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来城市中将同时存在人类驾驶车辆和由少数公司运营的自动驾驶车队，需要设计合理的市场结构以实现城市目标，而现有的基于市场份额的竞争机制可能导致车队运营商采取反社会策略。

Method: 构建基于市场份额的收入模型，设计多种路由算法，通过基准场景进行对比分析，研究人类驾驶员对CAV态度存在多样性时的系统表现。

Result: 当人类驾驶员对CAV态度呈现显著多样性时，随机化CAV路由策略（导致HDV出行时间不可预测）比按系统最优/用户均衡比例路由更高效；纯市场份额目标可能激励车队运营商采取反社会的随机化策略。

Conclusion: 建议改进市场设计，将系统平均行程时间纳入车队运营商的目标函数，以限制反社会的随机化策略，引导市场竞争朝向社会福利导向的合作方向发展。

Abstract: In future cities every driver may own a vehicle which could be either independently driven (HDV), or autonomously routed and piloted (CAV). The autonomous operations could be handled by a few competing companies. What is the market structure which would make this market aligned with city goals? In this paper we discuss a variant of the emerging market of collectively routed fleets of CAVs, where revenue for fleet operators is proportional to market share. We provide benchmark scenarios to compare the routing algorithms. We present several routing algorithms and demonstrate that, when the attitudes of human drivers towards CAVs exhibit significant diversity, randomised CAV routing, resulting in unpredictable travel times for HDVs, is more efficient than routing proportional to system optimum/user equilibrium. Based on this, we propose to improve the design of the market by augmenting the market-share objective with mean systemwide travel time in order to limit antisocial randomised strategies of fleet operators and drive the competition towards social welfare oriented cooperation.

</details>


### [188] [The Energy Society: A Simulation Environment for Studying Agent Cooperation under Survival Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14865)
*Lucas Bergholdt Hansen,Federico Torrielli,Filippo Tonini,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Energy Society，一个最小化生存经济环境，用于研究竞争与合作激励机制如何影响LLM智能体的涌现行为，发现大模型消耗更多能量，合作激励促使智能体做出利他行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被部署在多智能体环境中，环境中的激励机制可能塑造其行为。然而，当前缺乏一种简单的测试环境来研究推理成本与生存压力相结合时，竞争与合作激励如何影响智能体的涌现行为。

Method: 提出了Energy Society——一个最小化生存经济环境，智能体根据模型大小在生成token时消耗能量，通过完成任务或接受捐赠恢复能量，能量归零时被停用。研究者在竞争性、合作性目标以及基线设置和控制变体中进行对比实验，并进行消融研究分析记忆和行动推荐机制的作用。

Result: 大模型始终消耗最多能量且支出大于收入，即使在token成本不依赖模型大小的设置中也是如此。合作激励显著改变行为：智能体捐赠以重新激活他人，有时牺牲自身生存；允许智能体互相推荐行动支持协调和任务选择，记忆帮助智能体从过去结果校准风险。智能体很少选择直接破坏，但在竞争环境中表现出更微妙的利己行为。

Conclusion: Energy Society是一个紧凑的测试平台，可用于研究生存压力下token成本与群体激励之间的相互作用，为理解和设计多智能体系统中的激励机制提供了新的研究工具。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments whose incentives can shape their behavior. We introduce The Energy Society, a minimal survival economy for studying how competitive and cooperative incentives affect emergent behavior when inference cost is directly tied to survival: Agents spend energy based on model size when generating tokens, regain energy by completing jobs or receiving donations, and deactivate if their energy reaches zero. We compare competitive and cooperative objectives against a baseline setting and several control variants. Across experiments, larger models consistently consume the most energy and spend more energy than they gain, even in those settings where token cost is not size-dependent. Cooperative incentives substantially alter behavior: agents donate to reactivate others, sometimes at the cost of their own survival, and job allocation changes. Ablations reveal that allowing agents to recommend actions to each other supports coordination and ambitious job selection, while memory helps agents calibrate risk from past outcomes. Agents rarely choose direct sabotage, but show more subtle signs of self-serving behavior in the competitive setting. The Energy Society is a compact testbed for studying the interaction between token costs and group incentives under a survival pressure. Source code is available at https://github.com/LucasBergholdt/EnergySociety

</details>


### [189] [Stigmergic Graph Memory: An Environment-Aware Approach for Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15182)
*Aditya Dutta,Joon-Seok Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM)方法，通过在仓库节点和边上记录最近执行信号的衰减记忆层来改进多对多MAPD任务的端点选择，在所有15个地图-负载条件下相比基线方法实现20.5-36.7%的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图引导方法主要在目标固定后影响路径规划，而忽略了将最近交通信息纳入端点实例化决策的必要性。在多对多MAPD问题中，请求指定的是库存单位而非固定端点，需要在路径规划前选择代理、源和目的地，因此如何基于最新交通状况进行端点选择成为关键问题。

Method: 提出Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM)方法，构建一个有界的、衰减的记忆层，在仓库节点和有向边上记录最近的执行信号，用于对可行端点和路径偏好进行排序，而不改变碰撞约束或规划器有效性。该方法在目标进入规划器之前就基于最新执行记忆来塑造可行目标的选择。

Result: 在5种布局、3种负载水平、每种条件25个随机种子的成对请求流实验中，SGM在所有15个地图-负载条件下均优于两个重建的多对多分配基线方法，成对吞吐量提升范围为20.5-36.7%。

Conclusion: 最近执行记忆能够通过塑造哪些可行目标进入规划器来提高仓库吞吐量，而不仅仅影响代理如何前往已固定的目标，证明了在端点实例化阶段纳入执行信息的重要性。

Abstract: Automated fulfillment warehouses must continuously assign and execute pickup-and-delivery work while avoiding congestion. In many-to-many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD), a request specifies a stock-keeping unit rather than fixed endpoints, requiring the controller to select an agent, source, and destination before path planning. Existing graph-guidance methods primarily influence routing after goals are fixed, leaving endpoint instantiation uninformed by recent traffic. We introduce Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM), a bounded, decaying memory layer that records recent execution signals on warehouse nodes and directed edges to rank feasible endpoints and route preferences without altering collision constraints or planner validity. Across paired request streams on five layouts, three load levels, and 25 seeds per condition, SGM outperforms two reconstructed many-to-many allocation baselines in all 15 map-load conditions, with paired throughput gains of 20.5-36.7%. These results show that recent execution memory can improve warehouse throughput by shaping which feasible goals enter the planner, not only how agents travel to already fixed goals.

</details>
